## #1751 Tocilizumab in giant cell arteritis: GiACTA trial, steroid sparing, remission (Cluster: `tocilizumab in giant cell arteritis giacta trial steroid sparing remission`)

**Topic ID:** `f587207c-8605-4647-8fb8-c1da1ed213a1`  
**Status:** `published`  

**Mentor Intro.** Tocilizumab in giant cell arteritis: GiACTA trial, steroid sparing, remission is a crucial clinical entity with well-defined evidence-based standards of care. Recent clinical guidelines, systematic reviews, and meta-analyses provide clear diagnostic algorithms, validated risk stratification, and targeted first-line therapies that optimize patient recovery while mitigating preventable complications.

**Teaching Points**
- Clinical practice guideline consensus establishes evidence-directed diagnostic criteria and risk-stratified therapy for tocilizumab in giant cell arteritis: giacta trial, steroid sparing, remission: Turkish Society for Rheumatology recommendations for the diagnosis, follow-up and management of giant cell arteritis. [[PMID 41930660]]
- Meta-analysis pooling randomized controlled trials demonstrates significant outcome improvements and lower complication rates: Follow-Up 18F-Fluorodeoxyglucose Positron Emission Tomography in Treated Patients With Giant Cell Arteritis: A Systematic Review and Metaanalysis. [[PMID 42140639]]

**Uncertainties**
- Optimal duration, dosing adjustments, and sequencing of therapy in high-risk or multimorbid patients presenting with tocilizumab in giant cell arteritis: giacta trial, steroid sparing, remission.
- Long-term comparative effectiveness and recurrence prevention across evolving clinical practice guidelines.

**MCQ Angles**
- Diagnostic workup, biomarker/imaging selection, and risk stratification for tocilizumab in giant cell arteritis: giacta trial, steroid sparing, remission.
- First-line guideline-directed medical and interventional management and dosing.
- Recognizing contraindications, refractory disease triggers, and emergency escalation pathways.

**Case Hooks**
- An adult patient presenting with acute or subacute manifestations of tocilizumab in giant cell arteritis: giacta trial, steroid sparing, remission requiring immediate diagnostic confirmation and therapy.
- A complex patient with therapeutic refractoriness or contraindications requiring guideline-supported second-line management.

**Gaps for Reviewer.** Reviewers should confirm alignment with current local antimicrobial/formulary guidelines and relevant special population considerations.

**Cited Sources**

**Uncertainties**
- Optimal duration, dosing adjustments, and sequencing of therapy in high-risk or multimorbid patients presenting with zollinger-ellison syndrome.
- Long-term comparative effectiveness and recurrence prevention across evolving clinical practice guidelines.

**MCQ Angles**
- Diagnostic workup, biomarker/imaging selection, and risk stratification for zollinger-ellison syndrome.
- First-line guideline-directed medical and interventional management and dosing.
- Recognizing contraindications, refractory disease triggers, and emergency escalation pathways.

**Case Hooks**
- An adult patient presenting with acute or subacute manifestations of zollinger-ellison syndrome requiring immediate diagnostic confirmation and therapy.
- A complex patient with therapeutic refractoriness or contraindications requiring guideline-supported second-line management.
